# Retroactive look-first audit, Oct 27, 2026

The owner asked on Oct 27 for the look-first search to be run on everything already in flight. Two thirds of the open PRs should close: most repeat work already on main or answer a question he already ruled. One in five priority pool items is a twin, already built, or planned against a ruling. No question needs to go to him. Nothing was built or merged.

## Counts

**Open PRs: 191.**

| Measure                                                           | PRs |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------- | --- |
| Follow a ruling                                                   | 102 |
| Contradict a ruling, or ask a question one already answers        | 27  |
| Standing rules only (no specific ruling)                          | 62  |
| Duplicate code already on main (a merged PR or an identical file) | 83  |
| Duplicate another open PR                                         | 52  |
| Verdict: close                                                    | 128 |
| Verdict: send back                                                | 38  |
| Verdict: merge as is                                              | 25  |
| Verdict: needs the owner                                          | 0   |

The rows overlap, so the duplicate counts don't add up to 191. Most of the 128 closes are Oct 17 to 22 builder drafts with no push since Oct 22. Each one is already on main under another number, has zero changed files against main, or conflicts with main in 100 or more code files.

**Pool items, priority 1 or 2, open or in-pr: 479.**

| Measure                                                              | Items |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----- |
| Twins of another item                                                | 37    |
| Already built (on main, or done by a merged PR)                      | 40    |
| Roll-ups whose children carry all the work                           | 9     |
| Other close (superseded, can't be reproduced, rights gate withdrawn) | 13    |
| Contradicting a ruling, or not SMART (need correcting)               | 31    |
| Keep                                                                 | 349   |
| Needs the owner                                                      | 0     |

**Sessions:** 10 pieces of work done twice by two sessions, 10 pieces planned against a ruling, and 8 rebuilds of something already on main.

## How the rows were checked

Read: OWNER-DECISIONS (whole, through Oct 27 6:20 PM), DECISION-REGISTER (grepped by subject), prior-rulings/\*.md (grepped by subject), LAW, `playtest/2026-10-26-1.md` and `2026-10-27-1.md` (the helpers also read the sitting log); searched docs/, the pool and src/ for each claim. Drive: a Drive connector was listed but not searched, so anything ruled only in Drive and missing from the prior-rulings digests can be missed here. Main was at commit `c9c329c2c`.

Three read-only helpers each took one list, then I reviewed every Stop-now row:

- The 9 zero-diff PRs: all show zero changed files through the compare API.
- 12 "duplicate of a merged PR" pairs: each merged PR is confirmed merged, with the same subject.
- 7 pool verdicts, read against the item files.
- The sessions list's merged-PR claims: #6429, #6394, #6436, #6232, #6226, #6285 and #5971 are all merged.

Every sample held. Rows I did not sample carry the helper's confidence mark.

**One disagreement, settled by a ruling, not asked.** The sessions pass proposed asking the owner whether Issues may show real statute citations and enactment dates. OWNER-DECISIONS.md:12 answers it (Oct 27): "laws in force stay as the real starting law." OD:238 gives laws their enactment history, and OD:84 makes citations open the game's own display of the law. So statute citations and enactment dates of laws in force stay real, and only statistics get a per-world spread. `play-issues-real-statutes-and-dates` and `laws-bill-section-cites-research-source` are narrowed to statistics and survey titles. No question goes to the owner.

**What could not be delivered as ordered:**

- The order asked for the Stop-now list to go to the CTO session by `send_message`. The call was refused because that session needs a trusted device, so nothing was sent. The list is here and in the PR body.
- AGENTS.md ("Sessions never message each other") and OWNER-DECISIONS.md:315 also forbid that message. Only the scene line is excepted (OD:60).

## Who does what next

- **The CTO:** closes the PRs above, corrects MERGE-FIRST:15-16, records the two Oct 27 playtest rulings (opening introductions, "turns on"), and gives the inaugural and `gov-federal-questions-answers-stances` one owner each.
- **Mergers:** take the 25 merge-as-is PRs (end of table 1), #6424 first because it fixes red main.
- **This audit:** comments one `Look-first:` line on each PR that contradicts a ruling or duplicates main or another PR, and has written a Notes line in every twin, already-built and contradicting item (main, 5f4243b2f2f); the CTO or a merger sets them dropped.

The three tables below are the helpers' full output, reviewed. Each row gives (a) the ruling with FOLLOWS, CONTRADICTS or NO RULING, (b) whether the mechanism already exists, and (c) the verdict with a confidence mark.

## Table 1: open PRs

Read-only audit by a helper session. Nothing was commented, pushed, merged or edited. Sources read: AGENTS.md, docs/coordination/OWNER-DECISIONS.md (whole), MERGE-FIRST.md, docs/play-first/LAW.md, the Oct 26 and Oct 27 sitting notes, the pool item Notes for each PR, `LEADS-2026-10-24.md` where PR numbers appear, and the code on main. The DECISION-REGISTER and prior-rulings digests were grepped for the subjects below and held nothing on them (read-only history since Oct 25, OD:110). No owner-decision question is open: every claim a PR makes was answered by a ruling, a standing rule or the pool notes, so there are no NEEDS OWNER rows.

#### How to read it

- Citations: `OD:n` = docs/coordination/OWNER-DECISIONS.md line n; `AG:n` = AGENTS.md line n; `MF:n` = docs/coordination/MERGE-FIRST.md; `LAW:n` = docs/play-first/LAW.md; `PT26:n` / `PT27:n` = `docs/coordination/playtest/2026-10-26-1.md` / `2026-10-27-1.md`; `LEADS:n` = `docs/coordination/LEADS-2026-10-24.md`; `pool/<id>:n` = docs/pool/items/<id>.md line n (the Notes the mergers and audits wrote); `#N merged` = confirmed merged through the repo pull API (closed, merged=true).
- (a) FOLLOWS / CONTRADICTS / NO RULING (no ruling = only a standing rule or nothing). (b) names the file or merged PR when the mechanism is already on main, or the open PR that does the same. (c) the verdict, with confidence. Staleness in (c): `last push` is the tip commit date of the PR branch; `N code/M doc file conflicts` is from a read-only `git merge-tree` of the PR head against main (very high counts mean main has moved so far that the branch must be redone, not rebased).
- MF:22 (the CTO rule): an open PR with no push for five days that is conflicted or sent back is closed and its item set open. Today that means last push on or before Oct 22. I apply it where it is the only reason; otherwise the stronger reason is named first.
- I did not read every diff. Low-confidence rows say what I did not check.

## Table 3: priority 1 and 2 pool items

Read-only audit of 479 pool items (status open or in-pr, priority 1 or 2) on main at c9c329c2c (Oct 27, 23:15 UTC). Nothing was pushed, edited, commented or messaged. No tests were run (no node_modules in the worktree); every "built" claim is from reading files, stopgaps.json rows, git history and pool Notes, and says so in its confidence.

**Reading line.** Read: OWNER-DECISIONS (through Oct 27, 6:20 PM Eastern. Whole file), DECISION-REGISTER (searched by term), prior-rulings/ (README. Screens.md C9/C10 read. The rest searched by term), LAW (whole), playtest sitting files (listed, OD summaries read), AGENTS.md, look-first and smart-and-look-first. Searched docs/ and pool for: recount, LFS, partnership, budget season, estate tax, small-dollar, eviction, homeless, shelter, snapshot, union, graded/hold/shippable, license/rights, owner-decision, TODO bodies; Drive: no connector.

**Citation key.** OD:n = docs/coordination/OWNER-DECISIONS.md line n on this main; LAW:n = docs/play-first/LAW.md line n; REG:n = DECISION-REGISTER.md line n; AGENTS = the named section of AGENTS.md. (LAW.md quotes older OD line numbers; every OD:n below was re-read in the current file.) "Pieces play unless graded out" = OD:140 plus AGENTS "Standing owner rules / No hand-written player text" (owner, Oct 24).

**Verdict codes.** KEEP; CLOSE AS TWIN OF id; CLOSE ALREADY BUILT (file:function or stopgap row or merged PR); CLOSE (other reason, stated); CORRECT (premise contradicts a ruling; corrected direction stated); NEEDS OWNER (none qualified). Confidence in brackets: high, med, low.

**The three findings that matter most.**

1. The Oct 20 "authored parts held until graded" stance (OD:179, OD:158) was released on Oct 24 (OD:140; AGENTS) and batch grading ended Oct 27 (OD:64), but about twenty items still carry it. Eleven of them (the #5647 "slot is empty on main" follow-ups, mostly priority 1) ask for lines that already ship: journal.json, scene-words.json, bring-it-up.json, conversation.json, speech-stages.json all have shippable pieces. Three more build the batch-12 grading page the owner rejected on Oct 15 (OD:444).
2. Stopgap rows marked resolved and merged PRs already cover several large clusters. The unplaced-worker income cluster is resolved (SG-CORE-filled-unplaced-income, SG-CORE-unplaced-workers-income), and so are counted job choices (SG-WORLD-jobs-counted-choices). Budget adoption by vote is done (gov-stopgap-budgets-missing-engine), three of the budget season's four children are done, all four civic director producers are done, and the staff-candidate index is done (#5835).
3. A few items would undo rulings. Issues and bill sections would strip statute citations and enactment dates (OD:12, OD:238, OD:84 keep the law real). One adds a Play button on the Government screen (OD:82 puts play-as in the dev-mode start). Others are a law-teller scene (OD:538), a rights-review gate (OD:95), and two re-asks of owner rulings (OD:599, OD:192).
